Title: Analecta Bollandiana, tomus VII (1888), including Antony of Choziba, Vita
  sancti Georgii Chozibitae (the Life of George of Choziba), Greek text ed. C. Houze
Creator: Antony of Choziba (7th c.) and the other contributors to the volume (Société
  des Bollandistes)
Publisher: Brussels: Société des Bollandistes (Analecta Bollandiana 7), 1888
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: 1888 publication, out of US copyright by date; the Internet Archive item
  (a serials microfilm scan) carries no copyright field, so the imprint is the basis.
  Read directly 2026-10-16.
Language: grc (the Life is in Greek with Latin notes; the OCR of the Greek is rough and
  this file is SECOND WITNESS ONLY)
Source: Internet Archive item sim_analecta-bollandiana_1888_7
  (archive.org/details/sim_analecta-bollandiana_1888_7), fetched directly (curl, raw
  bytes) from the item's own _djvu.txt full-text file, 2026-10-16, Library thread
  (Era III).
Provenance note: Raw djvu OCR of a journal volume; Latin readable, Greek rough. Runs of
  2+ spaces collapsed; no wording changed. Sections: the journal's articles with page
  numbers; the Life by numbered chapters. Credit line: "Analecta Bollandiana 7 (1888)".
  Whole text republishable. No public-domain English exists (Vivian and Athanassakis
  1994 is in copyright).
Content note: The Life of George of Choziba is an insider's account from the Judean
  desert of the Persian invasion of 614 and of laura life at Choziba and Calamon.
